# France 5CA: readings to sign

Drafted by `tools/country_5ca.py` (src/country5ca.py) from the store's watched and tier-1 votes, by rules, never by a model. **Nothing here places anyone until it is confirmed**: automatically on Chris's delegation when every check passes (11 October 2026, `--auto-confirm`), otherwise by a named person. Every amendment and article vote on our ground is read and scored, as in every other country (Chris, 12 October 2026, lifting the limit of 10 October).

**To amend any reading** (a signer for fr in `config/stance_signers.yaml`): `python3 tools/country_5ca.py --cc fr --amend KEY --yea N --nay N --by NAME --why "..."`, or `--take-out` instead of the values to put it out of play. An amended reading is never touched by the automation again.

**How to sign.** Tick `[x]` on each reading you accept as drafted, then run `python3 tools/country_5ca.py --cc fr --sign-from-doc --by NAME` (it sets `status: confirmed` with `confirmed_by` and `confirmed_on` in `config/fr_stance.yaml`). To change a value, edit the stance file first. A reading that needs reading has no box: write `yea:`/`nay:` in the stance file, set `status: draft`, then confirm it.

Counts: 15 proposed, 0 procedural (evidence only), 1088 need reading, 3 confirmed (0 automatically).

### [ ] `senat-2025-337` sur l'ensemble de la proposition de loi visant à protéger les mineurs des risques auxquels les expose l'utilisation des réseaux sociaux

- **When / result:** 2026-07-21; Sénat; Pour 243, Contre 2, Abstain 100
- **On:** Protéger les mineurs des risques auxquels les expose l’utilisation des réseaux sociaux
- **Lobbies:** Pour: Les Républicains 129, UCI 58, Les Indépendants 20, RDPI 19, RDSE 17. Contre: NI 1, GEST 1. Abstain: SER 64, CRCE-K 18, GEST 15, NI 3
- Meaning: **Pour** = to pass DLR5L17N53187 (the under-15 social media rule and age checks) as a whole; **Contre** = against passing it. _(by rule, from the vote's wording and the bill)_
- **Proposed direction:** Pour -2 (Voted Pour on DLR5L17N53187 (Protect minors from the risks of social networks (ppl25-304): the under-15 social media ru): for passing it, against CitizenGO's position.); Contre +2 (Voted Contre on DLR5L17N53187 (Protect minors from the risks of social networks (ppl25-304): the under-15 social media ru): against passing it, with CitizenGO's position.)
- **Reasoning:** Area 7 (free speech and civil liberties). Bill direction on file for DLR5L17N53187 (CONFIRMED): passing it is AGAINST us -- Protect minors from the risks of social networks (ppl25-304): the under-15 social media rule and age checks. Passed the Senat on 21 July 2026. Matched nothing. This vote is a vote on the whole text or the motion itself, so Pour is against us.

## Confirmed

### [x] `senat-2025-313` sur l'ensemble de la proposition de loi visant à réparer les préjudices causés par la transplantation de mineurs de La Réunion en France hexagonale de 1962 à 19

- out of play: No CitizenGO stake (Chris, 12 Oct 2026): taxonomy misfile: 'transplantation' of children, not organs; confirmed by Christopher Joyce on 2026-10-12
- Meaning: **Pour** = to pass as a whole the bill providing redress for the harm caused by moving minors from La Réunion to mainland France between 1962 and 1984; **Contre** = to reject the bill providing redress for the harm caused by moving minors from La Réunion to mainland France between 1962 and 1984. _(read by the free session judge)_

### [x] `senat-2025-294` sur l'ensemble de la proposition de loi visant à garantir la neutralité financière du don d'organes et d'autres éléments et produits du corps humain par les viv

- out of play: No CitizenGO stake (Chris, 12 Oct 2026): organ donation: not contested ground; no stated position on donor cost neutrality; confirmed by Christopher Joyce on 2026-10-12
- Meaning: **Pour** = to pass as a whole the bill ensuring that living donors of organs and other body materials are not left out of pocket by donating; **Contre** = to reject the bill ensuring that living donors of organs and other body materials are not left out of pocket by donating. _(read by the free session judge)_

### [x] `an-17-5244` l'ensemble de la proposition de loi visant à réparer les préjudices causés par la transplantation de mineurs de La Réunion en France hexagonale de 1962 à 1984 (

- out of play: No CitizenGO stake (Chris, 12 Oct 2026): taxonomy misfile: 'transplantation' of children, not organs; confirmed by Christopher Joyce on 2026-10-12
- Meaning: **Pour** = passing at first reading the whole bill to make amends for the harm done by moving children from Réunion to mainland France between 1962 and 1984; **Contre** = voting against the whole bill to make amends for the harm done by moving children from Réunion to mainland France from 1962 to 1984. _(read by the free session judge)_

## Bill directions on file (inputs to the drafts; they place nobody)

- `DLR5L17N51670` against: Droit a l'aide a mourir (dossier 'Fin de vie'). Adopted in lecture definitive 15 July 2026, Conseil constitutionnel conforme 14 August, promulgated 18 August 2026. 380 scrutins name the dossier; 931 s (draft)
- `DLR5L17N51672` hold: Soins palliatifs et d'accompagnement, the companion bill to the aide a mourir law; promulgated 26 May 2026. Quebec has 'soins palliatifs' at tier 2 only. (needs_reading)
- `DLR5L16N49654` with: Senate bill to regulate medical practices for minors 'en questionnement de genre' (ppl23-435), adopted by the Senat and pending at the Assemblee since the 16th legislature. No Quebec term matches 'que (draft)
- `DLR5L17N53037` against: Universaliser l'assistance medicale a la procreation. France's legal term is 'assistance medicale a la procreation' (AMP); Quebec's list has 'procreation medicalement assistee'. (draft)
- `DLR5L17N51004` against: Faciliter la reconnaissance du genre a l'etat civil: self-identification at the civil register. Matched nothing. (draft)
- `DLR5L17N53403` against: Droits des personnes LGBTQIA+, intersex children and 'reconnaissance du genre a l'etat civil'. Matched nothing. (draft)
- `DLR5L17N53187` against: Protect minors from the risks of social networks (ppl25-304): the under-15 social media rule and age checks. Passed the Senat on 21 July 2026. Matched nothing. (confirmed)
- `DLR5L17N50627` with: Strengthens criminal law against child sexual abuse and exploitation; passing it protects children and fits CitizenGO's anti-exploitation stance. (confirmed)
- `an-17-4839` against: Officially recognises as a wrong the convictions and harm under France's pre-1975 abortion ban, framing legal protection of the unborn as injustice; passing it is against CitizenGO. (confirmed)
- `DLR5L16N49619` with: Bans conspicuous religious symbols, chiefly the headscarf, in sports competitions; Chris ruled on 12 October 2026 that religious-symbol bans are all with us, veils included (reversing his 11 October c (confirmed)
- `DLR5L17N51351` against: Senate version of the bill recognising as a wrong the harm suffered by women convicted under France's pre-1975 abortion ban; passing it treats protection of the unborn as injustice. (confirmed)
- `senat-2025-185` with: Bans buying personalised virtual sexual services and tackles online pimping; passing it fights the commercial sexual exploitation CitizenGO opposes. (confirmed)
